18                                                                                                            18
     APPENDIX R: C OMPARATIVE D ESIGNS AND C OMPLETE E CONOMIC R ECORDS
19                                                                                                            19

20        This section records the two comparative designs fixed after the reviewed ex- 20
21   periment and their complete economic outcomes. The strengthened-HJB family                               21

22   retains all 64 trials and 320 policy outputs; the continuation menu retains all 128                      22

23   trials, 640 method fits, and 1,920 stage candidates. Every declared event is included,                   23

24   independently of its sign. The numerical sources and complete raw evidence are                           24

25   immutable. The article interprets their final-stage or calibration-level comparisons;                    25

26   the following tables additionally preserve all stages, scalar statements, failures, and                  26

27   work accounts.                                                                                           27

28                                                                                                            28

29
                       R.1. A strengthened HJB family and economic variation                                  29

30        The strengthened baseline uses the exact covariance contraction of the Hessian                      30

31   of its two-layer neural value function, in both dimensions ten and fifty. For a                          31

32   hidden preactivation with input Jacobian J and covariance-contracted Hessian r, the                      32
                                       NBO SUPPLEMENT                                  171

1    componentwise transformation v = tanh(z) has Jacobian (1 − v2 ) J and contracted         1

2    Hessian                                                                                  2

3                                                                                             3
                            (1 − v2 )r − 2v(1 − v2 ) diag( JΣΣ⊤ J ⊤ ).
4                                                                                             4

5    Propagating these quantities through the architecture avoids a stochastic trace in the   5

6    fitting criterion. Independent automatic differentiation checks both the contractions    6

7    and their parameter gradients, including the nonzero terminal lift. Separate two-,       7

8    eight-, and thirty-two-probe diagnostics compare the original estimator and an           8

9    idiosyncratic Rademacher estimator with exact common-shock contraction. These            9

10   diagnostics do not choose the fitted baseline.                                           10

11     Four fixed candidate configurations cross occupation or mixed occupation/broad         11

12   collocation with Adam learning rates 0.0015 and 0.0005. Each has a maximum of            12

13   1,200 Adam updates and a subsequent L-BFGS stage of at most 150 iterations and           13

14   220 evaluations. Registered residual checks can stop a candidate after at least 400      14

15   Adam updates. Selection minimizes the equally weighted exact residual RMS on             15

16   two independent selection banks, with a deterministic tie rule. Final payoff, final      16

17   certification, untouched residual audits, probe variance, and the other methods’ 17
18   relative outcomes are excluded from selection. Residual-based selection is not a         18

19   uniform PDE error certificate.                                                           19

20     The selected value function has two prespecified deployments, both reported            20

21   without final-payoff selection between them. Greedy deployment uses a safe- 21
22   guarded Newton solution of the scalar mean-action fixed point, a maximum of              22

23   sixteen Newton steps, and a charged bisection fallback. Distilled deployment trains      23

24   a width-32 actor on cached greedy labels, with the same materialized actor capacity      24

25   as NBO, Raw, and direct policy optimization. Both deployments are charged the full       25

26   shared baseline construction, all four candidate fits, residual checks, diagnostics,     26

27   loading, final evaluation, and durable output. Shared work is disclosed rather than      27

28   hidden by selecting the cheaper deployment after seeing its payoff.                      28

29     The finite economic family contains four designs. The first two retain the his- 29
30   torical high and low volatility capital primitives. The third doubles the planning       30

31   horizon with intermediate volatility. The fourth jointly changes coupling, terminal      31

32   dispersion preference, the action radius, and initial heterogeneity. The latter is a     32
     172

1    stress design, not a causal estimate for a single changed primitive. Every design         1

2    is evaluated in dimensions ten and fifty over eight declared new training streams. 2
3    All five policy outputs—NBO, cached Raw, direct policy, greedy HJB, and distilled         3

4    HJB—have their own independent final evaluation. The 0.01 confidence budget               4

5    covers 648 declared events. Missing or failed work cannot be replaced by a favorable      5

6    seed. Complete fixed-envelope work here is distinct from the original economic            6

7    online-stopping experiment.                                                               7

8                                                                                              8

9                                                                                              9
                       R.2. A five-method continuation-reuse comparison
10                                                                                             10

11     The finite-period capital decision study retains the common continuation across         11

12   current utility weights, adjustment costs, and temporary action constraints. Four eco- 12
13   nomic designs comprise the original low-volatility anchor, the disclosed quarterly- 13
14   reuse pilot primitives, a longer shared continuation without an outcome pilot,            14

15   and an untouched intermediate design. Their stored coefficients define the finite         15

16   models exactly. No quarterly finite-model payoff is identified with the original          16

17   continuous-time diffusion without a separate transfer argument.                           17

18     Every calibration and dimension uses the complete equal-weight population               18

19   of sixteen prospectively fixed streams and every one of three work stages. The            19

20   five methods are a scalar NBO continuation, a vector costate representation, a            20

21   cached Raw actor, a task-conditioned direct actor, and cached Raw sample-average          21

22   optimization. Each is allowed legitimate reuse. In particular, changing a task does       22

23   not require a fresh innovation array; changing the postdecision state does require        23

24   reevaluating a cached payoff or derivative unless an approximation rule is specified. 24
25   The common query and task catalog is fixed before confirmation, and every selected        25

26   action precedes its independent payoff bank.                                              26

27     The primary family has 216 two-sided paired-payoff events, with probability             27

28   budget 0.018 and economic margin 10−4 . A separate 0.002 family has 128 scalar            28

29   whole-segment accuracy events, each using 65,536 independent antithetic pairs. The        29

30   query-conditional range and curvature results in the preceding section determine          30

31   these intervals. All stages, failures, original raw observations, numerical allowances,   31

32   and complete work are retained. A data-free power calculation uses analytic action- 32
                                         NBO SUPPLEMENT                                       173

1    distance fixtures rather than fitted candidates, so it cannot select a sample size after       1

2    observing the desired sign.                                                                    2

3                                                                                                   3

4                                R.3. Probability and chronology                                    4

5                                                                                                   5
       The four new families have budgets 0.01 for paired replication, 0.01 for economic
6
     robustness, 0.01 for the signed mechanism, and 0.02 for the continuation menu. 6
7                                                                                                   7
     Their union bound is 0.05, without assuming independence across families. Unused
8                                                                                                   8
     allocation is not recycled. Historical confidence statements retain their original
9                                                                                                   9
     families and are not silently pooled into this new union.
10                                                                                                  10
       The original statistical interval, a later deterministic refinement on the same old
11                                                                                                  11
     event, and a prospective new-bank confirmation have different chronologies. All
12                                                                                                  12
     three remain visible. A new bank for fixed policies does not constitute retraining;
13
     new training streams do not by themselves constitute new economic primitives. 13
14                                                                                                  14
     Complete work through a fixed computational envelope is not actual work to
15                                                                                                  15
     a successfully certified online target. These distinctions apply even when two
16                                                                                                  16
     displayed point estimates are numerically close.
17                                                                                                  17

18                R.4. Complete strengthened-HJB and cross-calibration outcomes                     18

     family with error probability 0.018. Current reward and a known terminal quadratic are removed

     plement. The preceding reading copy and complete numerical record are preserved 13

15   exact main and supplementary roots have Git blob identities, respectively,          15

       The revision’s source inventory records every source file and its digest in that
19                                                                                                                19
     reading copy. Its editorial map assigns every preceding equation, result, section, and
20                                                                                                                20
     table label to the current main text, current supplement, or the exact archived record.
